    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 34,\n  \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS) deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant. \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity: Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026) achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes: \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity) needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will be considered.\"\n}"

    source_document: DLN_P1_AandC1_157.PDF
    pdf_pages:
    - 1
    quote: "- **Amends:** [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 12 — Physical Submission of Original Tender Security|NIT Key Detail 12]]\n- **Change type:** Replace submission deadline\n- **Old deadline:** 16.10.2026 up to 18.00 Hrs IST\n- **New deadline:** 23.10.2026 up to 18.00 Hrs IST\n- **Submission methods shown:** In person, by post or by courier\n- **Evidence:** `DLN_P1_AandC1_157.PDF`, PDF page 1, amendment serial 1\n- **Affected booklet page:** 001\n\nThe source refers to the original Bank Guarantee (Physical), Insurance Surety Bond, Demand Draft or Banker's Cheque towards Tender Security (EMD).\n\nThe venue shown in both the existing and replacement columns is:\n\n> General Manager (Contracts)  \n> Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited  \n> 3rd Floor, BMTC Complex  \n> Shanthinagar, Bengaluru-560027  \n> Karnataka, India  \n> Email: contracts@bmrc.co.in"
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

- kind: summary
  text: To provide tenderers with reasonable preparation time after issuing addenda or corrigenda, the Employer may exercise discretion to extend the tender submission deadline in accordance with ITT Clause 15.0.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jef33ad2d05217f24
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C0436bff5854eed86
  citations:
  - id: Jef33ad2d05217f24
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 7.0 — Amendment to Tender Documents
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 13
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/6
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"7.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\\n\\n  7.1        Before the deadline for submission of Tenders, the Employer may issue further instructions\\n               to Tenderers or any modifications to existing Tender documents in the form of an addendum\\n         & corrigendum. Such an amendment in the form of an addendum/corrigendum will be made\\n              available at the eProcurement Portal. Bidders are advised to visit the Portal frequently.\\n\\n  7.2        Without prejudice to the order of preference as specified in Clause 1.5 of the General\\n             Conditions of Contract, the provisions in such addenda and/or corrigenda shall take priority\\n             over the Tender Documents issued previously.\\n\\n  7.3      To afford Tenderers reasonable time for preparing their Tenders after considering such\\n           amendments, the Employer may, at his discretion, extend the deadline for the submission of\\n             tenders in accordance with Clause 15.0 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match
  created_at: '2026-11-01T06:32:09+00:00'

- kind: summary
  text: No modifications or withdrawals of tender are permitted after the official deadline for submission, and post-deadline withdrawal triggers actions under ITT Clause 13.7.
  evidence_ids:
  - J1baf1e129a1708a7
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C48479f03e06ce9a0
  citations:
  - id: J1baf1e129a1708a7
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 17.0 — Modification and Withdrawal of Tenders
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 19
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/16
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"17.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"17.0     MODIFICATION AND WITHDRAWAL OF TENDERS\\n\\n  17.1      Except where expressly permitted by these Instructions, the Tenderer shall not make or cause\\n           any alteration, erasure, or obliteration to the text of the documents prepared by the Employer\\n           and submitted by the Tenderer with or as part of his Tender.\\n\\n  17.2     No modifications or withdrawal of tender shall be permitted after the deadline for submission\\n               of the Tender. Withdrawal of the Tender after the deadline for submission of Tenders but\\n             before the period of Tender Validity shall result in Employer’s action as per Clause 13.7 of\\n               Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  17.3     The Tender submitted online will be taken as the final bid.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"17.0     MODIFICATION AND WITHDRAWAL OF TENDERS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

# Tender Timeline - NIT Key Details and Amendments

> Draft source synthesis. Pending PDF and human review; no compliance or applicability decision.

## Summary

The baseline tender document availability period runs from 09.09.2026 to 09.10.2026 up to 3.00 PM IST, subsequently amended by Corrigendum No. 1 and Corrigendum No. 3. [^e1] [^e2] [^e3] [^e4] [^e5] [^e6] [^e7]

Corrigendum No. 1 modifies key schedule dates for the tender, extending the tender document availability, online submission window, technical bid opening date, and original tender security submission deadline. [^e8] [^e9] [^e10] [^e5]

## Additional source statements

- Addendum and Corrigendum No. 3 issued on 13.10.2026 further extends critical tender submission and opening dates, explicitly amending serial 1 of Addendum and Corrigendum No. 1. [^e16] [^e7] [^e28] [^e22]
- To provide tenderers with reasonable preparation time after issuing addenda or corrigenda, the Employer may exercise discretion to extend the tender submission deadline in accordance with ITT Clause 15.0. [^e64]
- No modifications or withdrawals of tender are permitted after the official deadline for submission, and post-deadline withdrawal triggers actions under ITT Clause 13.7. [^e65]

> {
>   "pdf_page": 34,
>   "text": "The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \nInvitation. \n8) \nIn case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other Government Body, Contract \nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS) deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant. \n9) \nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be considered. \n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification criteria: \nSl. No \nCriteria Requirement \nDocument Submission \nrequirement \n3.1.B (i) \nLiquidity: Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \navailability of credit facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \nfrom banks for meeting the fund requirement etc.). \nAttachment-13 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n3.1.B (ii) \nAnnual Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \nto 2025-2026) achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \nclasses of civil engineering construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \nAttachment-8 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n      Notes: \n1) \nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity) needs to be uploaded in the \nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \n2) \nAnnual financial statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial years \n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer must be certified either by the \nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \nhis stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number. \n3) \nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \nin the Audited Balance sheet will be considered."
> }

### Jef33ad2d05217f24

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 7.0 — Amendment to Tender Documents|ITT 7.0]], `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`, PDF p. 13 (`parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/6`)

> {
>   "clause_id": "7.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 13,
>       "text": "7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS\n\n  7.1        Before the deadline for submission of Tenders, the Employer may issue further instructions\n               to Tenderers or any modifications to existing Tender documents in the form of an addendum\n         & corrigendum. Such an amendment in the form of an addendum/corrigendum will be made\n              available at the eProcurement Portal. Bidders are advised to visit the Portal frequently.\n\n  7.2        Without prejudice to the order of preference as specified in Clause 1.5 of the General\n             Conditions of Contract, the provisions in such addenda and/or corrigenda shall take priority\n             over the Tender Documents issued previously.\n\n  7.3      To afford Tenderers reasonable time for preparing their Tenders after considering such\n           amendments, the Employer may, at his discretion, extend the deadline for the submission of\n             tenders in accordance with Clause 15.0 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT)."
>     }
>   ],
>   "heading_line": "7.0     AMENDMENT TO TENDER DOCUMENTS",
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review"
> }

### J1baf1e129a1708a7

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 17.0 — Modification and Withdrawal of Tenders|ITT 17.0]], `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`, PDF p. 19 (`parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/16`)

> {
>   "clause_id": "17.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 19,
>       "text": "17.0     MODIFICATION AND WITHDRAWAL OF TENDERS\n\n  17.1      Except where expressly permitted by these Instructions, the Tenderer shall not make or cause\n           any alteration, erasure, or obliteration to the text of the documents prepared by the Employer\n           and submitted by the Tenderer with or as part of his Tender.\n\n  17.2     No modifications or withdrawal of tender shall be permitted after the deadline for submission\n               of the Tender. Withdrawal of the Tender after the deadline for submission of Tenders but\n             before the period of Tender Validity shall result in Employer’s action as per Clause 13.7 of\n               Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\n\n  17.3     The Tender submitted online will be taken as the final bid."
>     }
>   ],
